# E-CIPM Index of Jurors — letter G

Full captured text of Mapping the Medieval Countryside's letter-G juror index,
accessed 22 June 2026:
<https://inquisitionspostmortem.ac.uk/browse/people/jurors-g/index.html>.

The capture is retained as source text for exact-name and variant lookup. It
includes many unrelated jurors; interpretation of the Gurnay/Gournay entries
belongs in the related research and E-CIPM extract files.
